# Autosegmental changes

## ˈkomojnis ‘mean’

### SPORADIC: the stress of *gamainiz falls on the root, after the prefix

```
       xo    -    moj - niz
       ╪
stress: primary
```

### SPORADIC: the stress of *gamainiz falls on the root, after the prefix

```
xo    -    ˈmoj   -    niz
            ┊
     stress: primary
```

### OE ǣ raises to ME /ɛː/

```
je    -    ˈmɛː  -     ne
            ┊
     stress: primary
```

### The Great Vowel Shift

```
     ˈmeːn
       ┊
stress: primary
```

### The MEAT vowel joins MEET: /eː/ raises to /iː/

```
     ˈmiːn
       ┊
stress: primary
```

### FACE, PRICE, FLEECE end in /j/, and PRICE starts at /ɑ/

```
     ˈmɪjn
       ┊
stress: primary
```

```
      ˈnʊt
       ┊
stress: primary
```

### The FOOT–STRUT split: short /ʊ/ stays after a labial before /l/, else becomes /ʌ/

```
      ˈnʌt
       ┊
stress: primary
```

## ˈswɑlwɔ̃ː ‘swallow’

### OE æ and ɑ merge on ME /a/

```
      ˈswa   -    lwe
       ┊
stress: primary
```

### The ME diphthongs [æi], [au] and [ɔu] are written [æɪ], [aʊ] and [oʊ]

```
      ˈswa  -     ʊw
       ┊
stress: primary
```

### *au before a labial becomes *æː (calf, half, salve)

```
     ˈswæːw
       ┊
stress: primary
```

### The BATH vowel backs to /ɑː/

```
     ˈswɑːw
       ┊
stress: primary
```

## ˈskɑwtɑz ‘sheet’

### The Old English diphthong ēa smooths to /ɛː/

```
     ˈʃɛːt
       ┊
stress: primary
```

### The Great Vowel Shift

```
     ˈʃeːt
       ┊
stress: primary
```

### The MEAT vowel joins MEET: /eː/ raises to /iː/

```
     ˈʃiːt
       ┊
stress: primary
```

### FACE, PRICE, FLEECE end in /j/, and PRICE starts at /ɑ/

```
     ˈʃɪjt
       ┊
stress: primary
```

## ˈɑskɔ̃ː ‘ash’

### OE æ and ɑ merge on ME /a/

```
      ˈas   -     ke
       ┊
stress: primary
```

### ME /a/ becomes Early Modern /æ/

```
      ˈæsk
       ┊
stress: primary
```

### TRAP lengthens before a voiceless fricative in the coda (BATH)

```
     ˈæːsk
       ┊
stress: primary
```

### The BATH vowel backs to /ɑː/

```
     ˈɑːsk
       ┊
stress: primary
```

## ˈbɑɣmɑz ‘beam’

### The ME diphthongs [æi], [au] and [ɔu] are written [æɪ], [aʊ] and [oʊ]

```
      ˈbæ   -     ɪm
       ┊
stress: primary
```

### The ME diphthong [æɪ] monophthongises and merges with ME ā

```
     ˈbɛːm
       ┊
stress: primary
```

### FACE: /ɛː/ diphthongises to /eɪ/

```
      ˈbe   -     ɪm
       ┊
stress: primary
```

### Short /e/ opens to /ɛ/

```
     ˈbɛjm
       ┊
stress: primary
```

## ˈɑrxʷɔː ‘arrow’

### Breaking: front vowels diphthongise before r/l + C and before x

```
       ˈæ   -     ɑr-  xu
       ┊
stress: primary
```

### The OE diphthongs smooth

```
      ˈɑrx
       ┊
stress: primary
```

### OE æ and ɑ merge on ME /a/

```
      ˈarx
       ┊
stress: primary
```

### ME /a/ becomes Early Modern /æ/

```
      ˈærx
       ┊
stress: primary
```

### Short /a/ backs to /ɑ/ before /ɹ/

```
      ˈɑɹ
       ┊
stress: primary
```

## ˈsurɣɔː ‘sorrow’

### NORTH: a short /ɔ/ before coda /r/ lengthens

```
     ˈsɔːɹg
       ┊
stress: primary
```

### THOUGHT rises to /oː/

```
     ˈsoːg
       ┊
stress: primary
```

## ˈnɑrwɑz ‘narrow’

### OE æ and ɑ merge on ME /a/

```
      ˈna   -     ru
       ┊
stress: primary
```

### The Great Vowel Shift: ME ā fronts and raises to [ɛː]

```
     ˈnɛːr
       ┊
stress: primary
```

### NEAR, SQUARE, CURE: a long vowel before /ɹ/ takes a schwa offglide

```
      ˈnɛ   -     əɹ
       ┊
stress: primary
```

### SQUARE and NEAR lose the schwa: /ɛə/ > /ɛː/, /ɪə/ > /ɪː/

```
      ˈnɛː
       ┊
stress: primary
```

## ˈtɑjxʷɔ̃ː ‘toe’

```
       ┊
stress: primary
```

```
      ˈtoː
       ┊
stress: primary
```

### The GOAT vowel: /oː/ diphthongises to /əʊ/

```
      ˈtə   -     ʊ
```
```
       ┊
stress: primary
```

## ˈxɑrβistɑz ‘harvest’

### Breaking: front vowels diphthongise before r/l + C and before x

```
      ˈxæ   -     ɑr - fist
       ┊
stress: primary
```

### Anglian smoothing of the i-mutated broken diphthong to /e/

```
      ˈxer   -    fist
       ┊
stress: primary
```

### Short /e/ and /o/ open to /ɛ/ and /ɔ/

```
      ˈxɛr   -    fɛst
       ┊
stress: primary
```

### NURSE: a stressed short vowel before coda /r/ becomes /əː/

```
     ˈhəːɹ   -    vəst
       ┊
stress: primary
```
